ivvāṟu nam āṅkilattāḷkaḷum āṅkila eḻuttāḷarkaḷum tamiḻukkē ciṟappoliyuṭiya coṟkaḷi oli kalantum vaṭivu kalantum eḻutiṉāl akkāṭci kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum iṉpuṟuttumā? tuṉpuṟuttumā?
moḻi nērmi
tamiḻttāy eṉṉa kēṭkiṉṟāḷ? tamiḻ makaṉ ayal moḻikkuk kāṭṭum moḻi nērmiyit taṉakkum kāṭṭumāṟu maṉṟāṭukiṉṟāḷ; vaḷarum ayalmoḻiyil kākkattuṭikkum moḻiyamippi, vaḷarnta taṉmoḻiyilum kāttuk koḷḷumāṟu paṇikkiṉṟāḷ. olip perukkam vaḷarcci yākātu. olipalkiya vaṭamoḻi mutalāṉa intiya moḻikaḷ evvaḷavō uḷavē; vaḷarntuḷḷaṉavā? vaḷarccikku nūṟperukkamum kalvip perukkamum cintaṉip perukkamum ivaṟṟiyellām naṉku matikkum makkaṭ perukkamum aracuc cārpumē tēvi.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭa varalāṟṟik kūrntu aṟinta varkaḷ ayaloliyi evvaḷavu vaṟpuṟuttit tiṇippiṉum tamiḻ ēṟṟuk koḷvatupōl kāṭṭit taḷḷiviṭuvati aṟivar. vāyuṭ pukuntu oṭṭiya oru ciṟu vēṟṟup poruḷiyum nā vaḷittut tuḷittu urāyntu eppaṭiyum veḷi yēṟṟuvatupōla, tamiḻum cilar ceyyuṭkaḷil cilar uṭinaṭikaḷil avvappōtu pukunta vēṟṟolikaḷik kālap pōkkil otukki viṭṭuviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvuṇmiyi aṟinta pavaṇantiyār vaṭacoṟkaḷ pala vāykkāl vaḻivanta iṭikkālattu anta vaṭavolikaḷ iṉṉiṉṉavāṟu tamiḻmi peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu virivilakkaṇam ceytār. vīramāmuṉivar tamiḻt tōṟṟam peṟṟu vāḻntatupōla, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻukku varumpōtu tammuruvatti akaṟṟik koṇṭu muḻut tamiḻuruvam peṟṟaṉa. ataṉāl accoṟkaḷ ōrāṟṟāl nilipēṟu eytiṉa. vaṭavilakkaṇaṅkaḷip peritum taḻuviya vīracōḻiyap puttamittiraṉārum tamiḻiṉ oliyamippukku ūṟu cey tārilli. nāyaṉmārkaḷ, āḻvārkaḷ, kampar, villi puttūrār, parañcōtiyār eṉṟa perumakkaḷ ellām tamiḻmi kuṉṟāta pāṭalkaḷ āyiramāyiram eḻutiṉar.
vēṇṭum collākkaṅkaḷ paṭitta piṉṉum, peyar vakiyālō piṟavakiyālō vēṟṟuc coṟkaḷi ōraḷavu taḻuvik koḷḷalām. itu vaḻivaḻi vanta tamiḻ marapē. aṅṅaṉam varum cila kiḷavikaḷ tam ayaṉmiyi viṭṭut tamiḻmi peṟṟu oṉṟiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatum oru peruntamiḻ marapu. oṭṭāc coṟkaḷum oṭṭāccoṟkaḷāl āya kaṭṭurikaḷum ceyyuḷkaḷum nūlkaḷum tamiḻiyaṟkikkup puṟampāṉavi ātaliṉ avi oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattaḷavukūṭa nili nillā eṉpataṉi nalla eḻuttāḷap perumakkaḷ maṉaṅ koḷa vēṇṭum. virintu varum putiya āytap pōkki niṉippiṉ ataṟku neṭum piṭippu illi. ciṉṉāḷil pal piṇiyuṟṟu maṟintuviṭap pōkiṉṟatu.
vantavāṟu eṅṅaṉē pōmāṟu ētō
māyamām itaṟkētum makiḻa vēṇṭām
cintiyīr umakkoṉṟu collak kēṇmiṉ
eṉpatu nāvukkaracar potuvākku. ātaliṉ eḻuttāḷarkaḷum ceytittāḷāḷarkaḷum taṅkay putiya karuttukkaḷit tamiḻiṉ niliyāṉa olikaḷit taḻuviyum eḻutuvārkaḷāka. putiya āytap pōkkik kiviṭuvārkaḷāka. vēṇṭumēl, pirāṉcu, pāram. puḷu. pilim, piliyōcā eṉṟu vanta ciṉ marapuppaṭiyāvatu eḻutuvārkaḷ eṉavum moḻi nērmiyit tamiḻaṉṉikkum pōṟṟit taruvarkaḷ eṉavum etir nōkkukiṉṟēṉ.


10. māṟṟoliyaṉ
tolkāppiyar tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ eḻuttu col poruḷ eṉṟa muttiṟattiṉiyum ārāynta paḻam pērilakkaṇar. avari ‘mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭi’ yavar eṉṟu pāyiram vitantu pukaḻkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl eḻuttiyal oliyiyal eṉṉum tuṟiyil avarkku irunta taṉivaṉmi aṟiyappaṭum. ivvaṉmikku eḻutattatikāramē kariyākum. cevippulaṉākiya olivaṭivu, kaṭpulaṉākiya varivaṭivu eṉa moḻikku iru vaṭivu uḷa. tolkāppiyar olivaṭiviyē mutaṉmiyākak koṇṭu ilakkaṇam eḻutiyavar. ‘ellā eḻuttum colluṅ kālip piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala’ (83) eṉṉum nūṟpā yāppil ‘colluṅkāli’ eṉpataṉ viṉip pakutiyi niṉika. nākarika vaḷarcci karutumiṭattu, moḻiyiṉ variniliyum vilakka muṭiyātākaliṉ varivaṭivu paṟṟiyum tolkāppiyar ōraḷavu moḻintaṉar.
olittuṟi
vāyoliyiyal (ñāṭinēviuḷa) moḻiyoliyiyal (ñāṭinēaiuḷa) eṉa olittuṟi irupāṟpaṭum. kaṇ maruttuvarkkum pal maruttuvarkkum kaṇ pal uṟuppukkaḷiṉ taṉikkūṟum taṉi maruttuvamum naṉku teriyum. orutuṟi pōkiya aṉṉavar uṭaliṉ potukkūṟukaḷiyum potu maruttuvattiyum kaṭṭāyam aṟintirukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. potunili aṟintārkkut tāṉē ciṟapput tuṟi naṉiviḷaṅkum. tolkāppiyar moḻiyoliñar; tamiḻ eṉṉum oru moḻiyiṉ olikaḷi ārāynta nuṇṇiyar; ‘eḻutteṉappaṭupa akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa’ eṉṟa mutaṟ pāvilēyē tamiḻiṉ oliyaṉkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi nūl toṭaṅkiyavar. oru moḻittuṟi muṭṭaṟutta ivar vāyoliñarum āvār. ‘uruviṉum iciyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum moḻik kuṟippellām eḻuttiṉ iyalā’  (40), ‘unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi’  (83), ‘ellā eḻuttum veḷippaṭakkiḷantu’ (102) eṉṟiṉṉa nūṟpākkaḷāl vāyolikaḷiṉ cila kūṟukaḷit tolkāppiyar kāṭṭic celvar. ittakiya irupēroliñar iyaṟṟiya eḻuttatikāram muṟṟum oliyatikāramātaliṉ, iṟṟiya oliñarkaḷiṉ kūriya pārvikku uriyatu. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarntu vaḻakkoṭuvarum tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ceṟinta olippaṇpukaḷit tolkāppiya vāyilākak kāṇpatum, kaṇṭu olittuṟi yārāyccikku utavuvatum nam kaṭaṉ.
oliyaṉ
oliyaṉ (Phoneme) eṉappaṭuvatu yātu? vēṟu teḷinta oli mutal nili eṉpar puḷūmpīlṭu. uṟavuṭiyaṉavum, colliṭit tamakkeṉat taṉi niṟpiṭaṉ uṭiyaṉavum ākiya oru moḻiyiṉ olik kuṭumpam eṉpar tāṉiyal cōṉcu. okkum piṟappiṭamum ovvā varaviṭamum uṭiya olikaḷiṉ iṉam eṉpar kiḷicaṉār.
“The confusion of usage with the term ‘Phoneme’ is unfortunately not unique in linguistic terminology. But in the case of the phoneme this confusion is particularly distressing becuse of the relatively recent introduction of the term” 
eṉṟu pirimeṉ tātal oliyaṉ ilakkaṇam paṟṟiya nīṇṭa āyvuriyil kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. (on defining the Phoneme - Reading in liguistics). itaṉāl vāymoḻikku mutalāṉa oliyaṉiṉ taṉmi mēlum kāṇṭaṟkuriyatu eṉpatum, itukāṟum palar varinta oliyaṉ ilakkaṇatti vittuk koṇṭu, tamiḻ olik kūṟukaḷit tuṇital kaṭi pōkātu eṉpatum pōtarum. oliyaṉ paṟṟit tolkāppiyattāl aṟiyalākum karuttuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟi viḷakkuvāṉ ikkaṭṭuri varikiṉṟēṉ.
	     1.		    2.
	kaṭam			māṉ
	caṭam			māṇ
	taṭam			māl
	paṭam			māḷ
	naṭam			māḻ
	maṭam			māy
mutaṟpakutiyil (k c t p n m) mutal meyyoliyiṉ vēṟupāṭṭālum, iraṇṭān tokutiyil (ṉ ṇ l ḷ ḻ y) īṟṟu meyyoliyiṉ vēṟupāṭṭālum coltōṟum poruḷ vēṟuvarak kāṇkiṉṟōm. .iv vēṟṟumikku olivēṟṟumiyē - avvoli vevvēṟāṉa paḷḷiyil (point of articulation) piṟappatuvē kāraṇamām. ātaliṉ poruḷ vēṟṟumi ceyyum piṟappu vēṟṟumiyuṭiya olikaḷi orumoḻiyiṉ oliyaṉkaḷ (Phonemes) eṉṟu terikiṉṟōm. poruḷaṭippaṭiyil oliyārāycci nikaḻtal kūṭātu eṉpatu oliñar pallōr kōṭpāṭāyiṉum akkōḷ ōrelli yaḷavē cellum. vāyoliyāyvukkē porunti varum. oru moḻippaṭuttum oliyi āyuṅkāli, poruṭpārvi nilikkaḷam ākum. ‘meyteri vaḷiyici’ (102) eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. mey-poruḷ. oru moḻiyiṉ oliyaṉ piṟitoru moḻiyil māṟṟoliyāka (allophone) irukkalām; oru moḻiyiṉ māṟṟoli maṟṟoru moḻiyil oliyaṉāka uyarntu viḷaṅkalām. ivvellām poruḷ paṟṟittāṉē aṟutiyiṭappaṭum.
eḻuttup pōli
nūṉmarapu moḻimarapu piṟappiyal eṉṉum mūṉṟiyalkaḷ eḻuttatikārattu mutaṟkaṇ uḷavē. ivi tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ olittiṟaṅkaḷi aṟivippaṉa. nūṉmarapu tamiḻ oliyaṉkaḷic cuṭṭi viḷakkukiṉṟatu. eḻuttu eṉṉum colliṟku oliyaṉ eṉpatuvē mutaṟporuḷ. moḻi marapil ivvoliyaṉkaḷ oru colliṭip paṭuṅkāl paṭum taṉmikaḷi viḷakkuvar āciriyar. piṟappiyalil ivvoliyaṉkaḷ piṟakkum paḷḷi kaḷiyum muyaṟcikaḷiyum taṉit taṉiyākap pulappaṭuttuvar. piṉṉar varum puṇariyalkaḷil tamiḻoliyaṉkaḷ coṟkaḷiṉ toṭariṭip paṭuṅkāl uṟum olittiripukaḷi virivāka aṟikiṉṟōm. iṉi moḻi marapiyalil colliya oliyaṉpaṟṟiya karuttoṉṟik kāṇpām.
1. 	aivaṉam 		- 	aivaṉam
2. 	aivaṉam 		- 	ayvaṉam
3. 	oḷavi 		- 	auvi
4. 	nāi 		- 	nāy
5. 	akaṉ	 	- 	akam
mēlik kāṭṭukkaḷip pōli allatu eḻuttuppōli eṉpar tamiḻnūlōr. ikkuṟiyīṭu tolkāppiyattu illi. itaṉi mutaṟkaṇ urikkiṭi āḷupavar iḷampūraṇarē. ‘mutalīṟiṭi nili pōli’ eṉṟu naṉṉūlār oli yiyalpukaḷi vakuttuk koḷvar. ip pōliyi uyirppōli, meyppōli, piṟapōli eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakiyākak koḷḷalām. aivaṉam - aivaṉam eṉpatu uyirppōli. akaṉ - akam eṉpatu meyppōli. aivaṉam - ayavaṉam; nāi - nāy eṉpaṉa piṟa pōli. oliyaṉ āvatu poruḷum piṟappum terintu teḷinta olimutaṟkūṟu. māṟṟoliyāvatu poruḷum piṟappum ottu, varavu (distribution) vēṟu uṭiya puṭiyoli. pōliyāvatu poruḷum varavum ottup piṟappu vēṟu uṭiya māṟṟoliyaṉ; aḵtāvatu ōr oliyaṉ niṟkumiṭattu vēṟu ōr oliyaṉ varum. itu poruḷ māṟṟañ ceyyātu oliyaṉi maṭṭum māṟṟi niṟṟaliṉ māṟṟoliyaṉ (allophoneme) eṉṟu peyar peṟum.
tamiḻ oliyaṉkaḷuḷ iṅṅaṉam oru collil vantu māṟṟi niṟkavalla oliyaṉkaḷit tolkāppiyar moḻi marapil eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar.
akara ikaram aikāram ākum   	(21)
akara ukaram oḷakāram ākum   	(22)
eṉṟa nūṟpākkaḷāl, ‘ai’ eṉṉum ōroliyaṉukku ‘ai’ eṉṉum iraṇṭoliyaṉkaḷum ‘oḷa’ eṉṉum ōroli yaṉukku ‘au’ eṉṉum iraṇṭoliyaṉkaḷum iṇintu vantu māṟṟoliyaṉ ākum. ‘ākum’ eṉṟu tolkāppiyar viṉimuṭipu ceytiruttaliṉ iraṇṭoliyaṉ kūṭi ōroliyaṉakku īṭākutal ceyaṟki eṉpatu karuttām.
akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum
iraṇṭu uyiroliyaṉkaḷ ōr uyiroliyaṉukku māṟṟāka īṭāka varuvati mēlē kaṇṭōm. innūṟ pāvāl uyirum meyyum (ay) iṇintu, aikārattiṉ māṟṟoli yaṉāka varuvatit taṉittu eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar. ‘ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum’ (58) eṉpataṉāl uyiroliyaṉukku meyyoliyaṉ māṟṟāka varuvatu kāṇka. ituvari viḷakkiya pōlikaḷ (ai, au, ay, y) ellām tattamakku uriya uyiroliyaṉkaḷiṉ (ai, oḷa, i) iciyōṭu otticippavi; cevippulaṉukku ōraṉṉa ōci tarupavi. ‘ai yottu icikkum’ ‘oḷa vōraṉṉa’ eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār pōliyiṉ ici yoppumiyi veḷippaṭuttuvar. ‘cila eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic cila eḻuttukkaḷ pōla icikkum eṉa eḻuttup pōli kūṟukiṉṟatu’ eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵteṉpa
pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa   	(82)
colliṭi oru meyyoliyaṉukku vēṟoru meyyoliyaṉ māṟṟoliyaṉāka varum eṉpatu ippōli nūṟpāviṉ karuttu (puṟaṉ - puṟam). ōr uyiroliyaṉukku iraṇṭu oliyaṉkaḷ māṟṟāka vanta nili meyyoliyaṉukku illi. uyirkku vanta māṟṟoliyaṉ avvuyirōciyōṭu ottu niṟpatupōla meykku vanta māṟṟoliyaṉ ammeyyōci yik kāṭṭi niṟpatumilli. pōliyuḷḷum ivvēṟupāṭu kuṟikkattakum.
pōli varampu
moḻikku oliyaṉkaḷ aṭippaṭiyāṉavi. ivaṟṟi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu muntu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟam.  ōroliyaṉukku māṟṟoliyaṉ varumeṉiṉ, oliyaṉiṉ taṉittaṉmiyum ciṟappum kuṉṟāvā? colniliyil ikarattukku yakaravoṟṟum ṉakarattukku makaramum varumāyiṉ, ivaṟṟi vēṟu vēṟu oliyaṉkaḷāka eṇṇuvatu piḻiyaṉṟō? ivi nalviṉākkaḷē. moḻiyil varum tiripukaḷukkellām oru peru varampu uṇṭu; atu poruḷ varampākum. ōroli kuṉṟal keṭal māṟal eṉat tiripupala uṟalām. ettiripu niliyilum, taṉakku uriya poruḷit tiriyātu taṉ moḻik kulattārkku (Speech community) teḷiyak kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. kāṭṭumāyiṉ, avvolittiripi ak kulattār poruṭpaṭuttuvatilli. aiyar - ayyar; viram - vayiram; oḷavi - avvi; aracu - aricu eṉavarum oliyaṉ māṟṟattāl muṉṉip poruḷ vēṟupaṭāmi kāṇka. ‘akarattu impar yakarap puḷḷiyum........ meypeṟat tōṉṟum’ (56) eṉṟu atē poruḷ tarutalit tolkāppiyar kuṟippar. ōroliyaṉukku varum māṟṟoliyaṉ ellāviṭattum vārā. varumāyiṉaṉṟō attakiya iraṇṭoliyaṉ iruppatu vīṇākum.
avaṉ 	- 	avam
araṉ 	- 	aram
nīlaṉ		- 	nīlam
kālaṉ 	- 	kālam
āyaṉ 	- 	āyam
kōlaṉ 	- 	kōlam
pālaṉ 	- 	pālam
iccoṟkaḷ nalla poruḷ vēṟupāṭu uṭiyavi. ātaliṉ ṉakaravoliyaṉum makaravoliyaṉum tam taṉittaṉmiyōṭu vantaṉavākavē iccoṟkaḷiṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum. eṉavē poruḷ māṟum eṉiṉ, pōli eṉṟa māṟṟoliyaṉukku iṭamilli eṉpatu tuṇipu.
iruvaki varici
oliyaṉ māṟṟattukkum oru varampu uṇṭu. atu poruḷ varampākum eṉṟu mēlē cuṭṭiṉēṉ. poruḷukku yātoru teḷivukkuṟivum ēṟpaṭātavāṟu, ēṟpaṭāta nilivari oliyaṉi māṟṟic collalāmā? māṟṟoliyaṉukku iṭaṅkoṭāc col variciyum iṭaṅkoṭukkum colvariciyum eṉa iruvaki tamiḻ moḻikkaṇṇē kāṇappaṭum.
	vili 			vāl
	viḷi 			vāḷ
	viḻi 			vāḻ
ivi iṭaṅkoṭāc colvarici., iccollamip pil mūṉṟu oliyaṉkaḷum (l ḷ ḻ) vanta coṟkaḷ iruttaliṉ, māṟṟoliyaṉ varavukku vāyppilli. kaṇ kaṉ, maṇ maṉ, māṇ māṉ eṉpaṉavum aṉṉa
	pukal 		kāl 		pāl
	pukaḻ 		kāḻ 			pāḻ
ivi iṭaṅ koṭukkum colvarici. iccolla mippil ḷakara oliyaṉ koṇṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil illi. ātaliṉ ḷakaram māṟṟoliyaṉākap pukutaṟkum pukaḷ, kāḷ, pāḷ eṉṟa coṟpōlikaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkum iṭaṉuṇṭu. ataṉālaṉṟō pavaḻam - pavaḷam; uḻuntu - uḷuntu; kaḻuttu - kaḷuttu eṉa māṟṟoliyaṉkaḷ vanta col vaḻakkāṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu. immāṟṟattāl poruḷ ūṟupaṭāmiyum kāṇka. ūṟupaṭata takka col muṉṉarē moḻikkaṇ iṉmiyum kāṇka. ḷakara ḻakara oliyaṉkaḷi makkaḷ poruṭpaṭutti oliyāmikku ituvē kāraṇamām.
maṅkalam : matil
iviyum māṟṟoliyaṉukku vāyppaḷikkum coṟkaḷ, iccollamippil ḷakara ḻakara oliyaṉkaḷuṭiya kiḷavikaḷ illi. ātaliṉ eppaṭi iccoṟkaḷi olippiṉum moḻik kulattuc cevikaḷ vāṅkik koṇṭaṉa. maṅkaḷam, matiḷ eṉṟa col vaṭivaṅkaḷi ilakkiyattum kāṇkiṉṟōm. uḻuntu uḷuntu, maṅkalam maṅkaḷam eṉṟa kāṭṭukkaḷāl ivvariciyil māṟṟoliyaṉāka varuvatu ḷakaramātal aṟiyattakum. muṉtōṉṟum lakarattukkum piṉtōṉṟum ḻakarattukkum iṭit tōṟṟamuṭiyatu ḷakaramēyākum.  ātaliṉ uluntu eṉavum maṅkaḻam eṉavum māṟṟoliyaṉ peṟāmikkuk kāraṇam peṟappaṭum. kili-kayili, muracu-muricu; putal-putar; ñāyiṟu-nāyiṟu; ñēyam-nēyam eṉṟa pōlikaḷ ellām mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭi iṭaṅ koṭukkum coṟkaḷil vantaviyē.
maṅkalam, maṅkaḷam; uḻuntu, uḷuntu; kuṭar, kuṭal eṉpaṉavaṟṟi oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi eṉak koḷḷalāmā? mali, vari, ōṅkal, aṭukkam eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi. iccoṟkaḷil oliyaṉkaḷ mika vēṟupaṭṭuḷa. maṅkalam, maṅkaḷam eṉpavaṟṟil pakka oliyaṉkaḷ ellām ottiruppa. ōriṭattuttāṉ oliyaṉ māṟṟam uṇṭu.; am māṟṟamum piṟappil aṇimiyuṭiyatu. ātaliṉ māṟṟoliyaṉ koṇṭa pōlic coṟkaḷ oru poruṭ paṉmoḻiyākā. oliyaṉkaḷ toṭarum oru collil - iṭaṅkoṭukkum oru colliṭi - māṟṟoliyaṉ varumātaliṉ, tolkāppiyar orumoḻittaṉmi kūṟum moḻi marapil pōli kuṟittu nūṟpākkaḷ yāttār. māṟṟoliyaṉ varavu pōliyēyātaliṉ, maṅkalam, aracu, muracu, mutalāṉa taṉṉoliyaṉuṭiya coṟkaḷ eṉṟum oru moḻiyil vāḻum; vīḻā eṉa aṟika. mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭiyavar tolkāppiyar eṉṟa  pārāṭṭiṉ meymmiyi aṟintu tolkāppiyatti muṉvittu nam moḻiyārāycciyit toṭaṅkuvōmāka.


11. pāvam
tamiḻmoḻi cila pattāṇṭukaḷākap pala tuṟiyilum taḻittup perukukiṉṟatu. urimi yuṇarcciyē ataṟkuk kāraṇam. viṭutaliyuṇarvō namakku moḻi yuṇarvōṭu tōṉṟiyatu. nam tāymoḻi tāḻātu vaḷara vēṇṭum; aracāḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa viḻiviyum viṭutalikku oru peruṅ kāraṇamāyp paṟicāṟṟiṉōm. pulavar perumakaṉ pāratiyār nāṭu moḻi eṉṉum iru perum paṟṟiyum oruṅku kuḻittu makkaṭku ūṭṭiṉār. mītūrnta moḻiyaṉpē āntirap piriviṉikku ētu eṉpatu veḷippaṭi.
ceytittāḷkaḷ vaḻakkut tamiḻiṉ vaḷarccikku naṟṟuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa. paṭakkāṭcikaḷ moḻiyip poṟuttavari, tamiḻākkattip peṇkaḷiṭattum kuḻantikaḷ iṭattum parappukiṉṟaṉa. itu kuṭiyaracuk kālam. makkaṭkuk karuttuk koḷuttavum, aṉṉavarit tañcārpāka iḻukkavum kāṭciyāḷarkaḷ paṟṟum potuk karuvi tamiḻ aṉṟō? immuṟiyāl tamiḻuyir nīṭittu viṭṭatu; ataṉ āṭci kālkoṇṭu viṭṭatu. uyarnilip paḷḷivari tamiḻmoḻi kalvimoḻiyāy aminta nāḷ toṭṭu, ataṉ celvākku “tamiḻil muṭiyumā?” eṉṟu iṭiyūṟāyk kiṭanta oru cārāri añci viyakkumpaṭi ceytatu. tamiḻakak kallūrikaḷum palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷum nilattut tamiḻiṉik karuttu vāyilākak koḷḷum kālam oṉṟu varum; akkālam aṇittu varuvatu aṟivuk kaṇṇukku atō tōṉṟukiṟatu. aññāṉṟu tamiḻ - uṭaṉ vāḻnta ulakat toṉmoḻi yellām vaḻakku oḻiyavum oḻiyā uyirt tamiḻ - ñāla araṅkil naṭam puriyak kāṇpōm.
moḻiyaṭippaṭiyil intiya nāṭu pakutippaṭṭu viṭṭatu. iṉi avvan nilattumoḻi parantōṅkic cemmākkum eṉpatu uṟuti. mēlum, tamiḻ vaḻaṅku nilam tamiḻakam maṭṭumaṉṟu. tilli, pampāy, pūṉā, kalkattā mutaliya intiyap piṟa nilaṅkaḷ tamiḻttāy kuṭikoṇṭirukkum putu māḷikikaḷ. malēyā tamiḻp piṅkūḻukku oru putuvayal. ilaṅki tamiḻaracuk kumariyiṉ araṇmaṉi. teṉṉāppirikkā tamiḻ naṅkiyiṉ uṟaviṭam. iv vaṇṇam paḻantamiḻ iṭattāṟ parantu ceyalāl nilittu vāḻvatik kāṇutoṟum kāṇutoṟum tamiḻ neñcaṅkaḷ vimmip pūrikkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟcolliyāṅku nam tāymoḻi palapaṭap paraviṉālum ataṉ vaḷarcci aṟiñar palarik kavaravilli. it tamiḻpperukkam niliyā? mēṟpōkkā? vēr ūṉṟumā? vaḻakkōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalakkumā? vaḷarvatu pōlak kāṭṭi, paḻiya perukkattiyum vārik koṇṭu pōyviṭumā? moḻiyaṭippaṭiyiyē takarttuc citittu urumāṟṟiviṭumā? ivvaḷarvukkut tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar poruntumā? eṉa ivvāṟu pala uḷḷaṅkaḷ ucāvi alikiṉṟaṉa. inta aiyaṅkaḷ mayakkattālō, poṟāmiyālō, cūḻcciyālō eḻuntaṉa valla. aṟivāl eḻunta aiyaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟukkut taliyāya oru kāraṇatti māttiram ikkaṭṭuriyiṟ kāṇpōm.
ilakkiyac cuviyip parappuvāruḷ oru cilar ‘pāvam’eṉṟa collic cila kālamākap peritum kiyāḷupa. uḷḷak kuṟippu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. tamiḻil muṉpē pāvam eṉap piṟitoru col uṇṭu. tīviṉi eṉpatu ataṟkup poruḷ. putiya kuṟippup pāvattip paḻiya tīviṉip pāvattiṉum vēṟu paṭuttuvataṟkup pāvattiṉ mutaleḻuttit taṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. ipparuveḻuttu moḻi muṟiyaṉṟu; valintu pukuttiṉālum kivaḻakkil oṭṭuvataṉṟu; tamiḻ varampu aṉṟu; ataṟku vaḷarcciyum aṉṟu. ōreḻuttiyē paṭṭiyākavum taṭṭiyākavum parumaṉākavum ciṟumaṉākavum cāyvākavum nērākavum kaṇṭavāṟu yār kiyum eḻutak kāṇkiṉṟōm. oḻuṅkillā inniliyil, parumi ciṟumi eṉṟiṉṉa oreḻuttiṉ tōṟṟa vēṟupāṭukaṭ kellām poruḷ vēṟu vēṟu kūṟap pukiṉ, emmoḻiyum viḷaṅkātu virivil iṭukāṭu aṭiyum.
ōr olikku ōreḻuttu eṉpatu moḻiyaṟam. oli vēṟu poruḷ vēṟu koṇṭāl eḻuttum vēṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. muṟi māṟi īrolikku ōreḻuttuk koḷvatum, avveḻuttiyē paṭṭi pōṭuvatum aṟappiḻi; moḻic citivu. inti vaṭamoḻikaḷum iṉavolikaḷukku vevvēṟu taṉiyeḻuttuk koṇṭaṉavē yaṉṟi, ōreḻuttuḷḷēyē viḷampum vēli ceyyavilli. eṇṇiṟanta vaṭamoḻik kiḷavikaḷ iṭikkālat tamiḻil pukuntaṉa. avvellām tam moḻimi yiḻantupukkakattu maṇamakaḷ pōlat tamiḻmi eytaṉa; tam oliyi viṭṭut tamiḻoli koṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉa.* ivviṉiya moḻiyākkamē nilippatu; piṉpaṟṟat takuvatu. tamiḻukku vantum kalavātu taṉittu niṟkum piṟa coṟkaḷ kālappōkkil tāmē akalum; allatu aṟivup puraṭciyāl akaṟṟappaṭum.
kuṟippu eṉṉum tamiḻc col muṉpē namakku uṇṭu. ataṉip pērāṭcikkuk koṇṭu varuvatu kaṟṟār kaṭaṉ, uriya col tamiḻil iruppavum, ‘pāvam’ eṉṟa piṟa moḻic col vēṇṭuvatilli. orukāl āḷavirumpiṉ, tamiḻp ‘pāvam’ āka oliyāmal vaṭamoḻip pāvam ākavē vittu olippatu poruntātu. vaḻakkil yārum eḻuti varum iyalpup pakara vaṭivāka eḻutāmal, parutta pakaramāka acciṭal evvāṟṟāṉum ovvātu. kaṭaṉ paṭṭālum varampu pōṟṟit tamiḻ vaḷarkkum vāḻvu namakku vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyar mutalāya cāṉṟōr tamiḻukku oliyilakkaṇam vakuttaṉarēyaṉṟi, eḻutuvār kikku eṟpa ettuṇiyō vēṟupaṭum variyilakkaṇam varintār allar. tamiḻukku aṭippaṭiyi oli niliyil kaṭṭiṉarē yaṉṟi varivaṭiviṟ kaṭṭiṉār allar. tamiḻ varampu ituvātaliṉ, ōreḻuttut taṉṉiyē taṭippittup poruḷ kāṭṭalum, varivaṭivit tamiḻ moḻikku uyirākkalum aṭimuraṇ eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
ipputup pāvac col paṟṟip piṟitoṉṟum niṉikka vēṇṭum. oru col nallatu tīyatu eṉa iru poruṭkum uriyatāyiṉ, kālappōkkil tīyatu nallati aḻikkum. ittīyaveṟṟi moḻiyaṟam; tamiḻ kāṭṭum varalāṟu. irutaṉmikkum uriyoru colli urikkum pōtu, kēṭpārkkut tīyaporuḷē muṉṉiṟkum ātaliṉ, nalla poruḷiṟ colla yārum tuṇiyār. talivaṉ poruḷīṭṭak kaḷḷik kāṭṭiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟa karuttik ‘kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭu iṟantōr’ (kuṟun-16) eṉpāḷ caṅkat talivi. ‘puli cettu verīiya pukarmuka vēḻam’ (akam-12) eṉṟa aṭikkaṇ, puli cettu-puli yeṉṟu karuti eṉpatu poruḷ. ‘māṇṭa eṉ maṉiviyoṭu makkaḷum nirampiṉar’ (puṟam. 191) eṉṟa toṭaril māṇṭa - ciṟanta eṉpatu poruḷ. ivvāṟu caṅkat tamiḻar naṟporuḷil vaḻaṅkiya iṟanta, cetta, māṇṭa eṉṟa coṟkaḷukku varavarac cāvup poruḷkaḷ ēṟiviṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, avaṟṟi nalla poruḷavāy makkaḷ āḷat tuṇiyavilli. avaṟṟiṉ naṟkūṟu vaḻakkoḻintatu. itu tamiḻ moḻi varalāṟu. ivviḷakkattāl nām aṟiyak kiṭappatu eṉṉa? pāvam eṉṟa putuc colli nalla kuṟippup poruḷāy iṉṟu nām āṇṭālum, paḻiya tīviṉip poruḷē kēṭpārkku muṉ tōṉṟum; kuṟippup poruḷ tāṉē oḻintupōm. ātalāl ipputiya pāvatti - parutta pāva eḻutti - tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar collāḷar piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭā eṉṟu vēṇṭikkoḷvaṉ. ikaliṉṟi ivvuṇmiyi ārāyntu koḷka.


12. ātticūṭiyum eḻuttiyalum
oḷaviyār pāṭiya ātticūṭi ētti ētti ārāytaṟku uriyatu. kāraṇam innūl ciṟitāyiṉum putumiyum oḻuṅkum uṭiyatu. ātticūṭikku urikaḷum katikaḷum palavuḷa; ilakkiya viḷakkaṅkaḷum camaya viḷakkaṅkaḷum pala tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. pāratiyār ātticūṭi, pāratitācaṉ ātticūṭi eṉa vaḻi nūlkaḷum eḻuntuḷa. āṅkila moḻi peyarppukkaḷum uṇṭu. piṟamoḻikaḷilum iccūṭi moḻi peyarkkappaṭum eṉṟu etirpārkkalām. nīti nūlkaḷil tirukkuṟaḷukku aṭutta ciṟappi ātticūṭi peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. vaḷḷuvarpōl oḷaviyum nāṭum vīṭum aṟiyap peṟṟavar.
ilakkaṇap pārvi
ātticūṭi palaniliyil ārāyappaṭṭiruntālum ilakkaṇa nōkkil eḻunta ārāycci illi. 108 ātticūṭikaḷum evvakippā ākum? cūttiram allatu nūṟpā eṉṟu peyariṭukiṉṟōm. ‘cūttiram yāppiṉuḷ tōṉṟa yāttamippatuvē’ eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvataṉṟi evvaki yāppu eṉṟu cuṭṭavilli. ‘cilvaki yeḻuttiṟ palvakip poruḷi’ eṉṟa nūṟpāvilum cūttirattiṉ taṉmi kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaki kuṟikkappaṭavilli. āciriyappāvilum veṇpāvilum kaṭṭaḷik kalit tuṟiyilum ilakkaṇa nūṟpākkaḷ amintiruppatik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ātticūṭiyuḷ nūṟu, aṟañceya virumpu, āṟuvatu ciṉam eṉa irucīrkaḷāl amintavi. ēṉi eṭṭum ṅappōl vaḷi, neṟpayir viḷi eṉa oru cīr koṇṭu mēlum ōraciyāl muṭipavi. āṉāl muḻutum orucīr ātticūṭiyō muccīr ātti cūṭiyō illi.
etuki mōṉi mutalāṉa toṭivikaṟpam paṟṟiyum ārāyvataṟku uriyatu icciṟu nūl. toṭi eṉpataṉ potuvilakkaṇam eṉṉa? ōr oḻuṅkāṉa iyakkam. nāṟcīrkaḷilum ōr oḻuṅku iruppiṉ muṟṟākum. iṟutic cīrkaḷiṉ oḻuṅku iyipām. mutaṟ cīrkaḷiṉ oḻuṅku mōṉiyām. antam mutalāt toṭuppatu antāti eṉṟu kāriki oru pāṭṭiṟkuḷ antātit toṭi vakukkiṉṟatu. ivvilakkaṇatti virivupaṭuttit tiruvāymoḻi pōṉṟa toṭarnilic ceyyuṭkaḷiyum antāti nūl eṉak koḷkiṉṟōm. ātticūṭiyiṉ amippilum oru moḻi yoḻuṅku uṇṭu. uyireḻuttukkaḷiyum meyyeḻuttuk kaḷiyum uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷiyum mutalākak koṇṭu 108 ceyyuṭkaḷi niralpaṭak kūṟukiṉṟatē; yāppil itaṟkoru peyar vikka vēṇṭāmā? ēṟkaṉavē uḷḷa peyar vakiyil itu aṭaṅkavilli. oḷavi ātticūṭiyum koṉṟi vēntaṉum piṉ eḻunta ātticūṭikaḷum neṭuṅkaṇakkut toṭiyuṭiyavi eṉṟu oru pututtoṭi koḷḷalām. ātticūṭiyiṉ ceyyuḷ toṭipaṟṟip piṉ eḻunta yāppilakkaṇaṅkaḷ karuttuc celuttavilli eṉpatu mēl eḻutiya cila kuṟippukkaḷāl pōtarum.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷ
ikkaṭṭuri ātticūṭi nūlil kāṇattakum moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ kuṟittu ōr oppīṭṭārāycci ceyya muṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyil kālantōṟum māṟum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum uḷa; māṟāta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum uḷa, māṟum ilakkaṇattiliruntu moḻivaralāṟu aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. eḻuttiyalil moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṭiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉa mūṉṟu nilikaḷ uḷa, tolkāppiyattilum naṉṉūlilum mutaleḻuttu iṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟi vēṟupāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. iṭinili mayakkam paṟṟi yātum māṟṟam illi. itaṉāl kālattil māṟum ilakkaṇak kūṟu etu, māṟātatu etu eṉṟu terintu teḷiya muṭikiṉṟatu. mutaṉiliyilum īṟṟu niliyilum paḻiyaṉa kaḻittup putiyaṉa taḻuviya naṉṉūlār iṭinili mayakkattil kālamāṟutali ēṟṟuk koṇṭilar.
iṇintiyal kāli yaralak kikaramum
mavvak kukaramum nakarak kakaramum
micivarum ravvaḻi yuvvu māmpiṟa 	(naṉ. 149)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṉ marapik kāttu valiyuṟuttuvar.
tamiḻ moḻi varalāṟṟil mutaleḻuttup paṟṟiya māṟutalkaḷi ārāyappukum nām tolkāppiyam vīra cōḻiyam nēminātam  naṉṉūl ivaṟṟiṉ urikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi mēṟkōḷākak koḷkiṉṟōm. mutaleḻuttiṉ ārāycci ilakkaṇa nūlaḷavil curuṅki niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvārāyccikku mutaleḻuttukkaḷiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu pāṭiya ātticūṭiyum ataṉi yotta koṉṟivēntaṉum uriya nūlkaḷ ām eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṟa īramippum koṇṭa ātticūṭiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu amippu piṉvarumāṟu. koṉṟivēntaṉ amippiyum uṭaṉ kāṇalām.
ātticūṭiyiṉ ceyyuṭkaḷ 108.
koṉṟi vēntaṉ ceyyuṭkaḷ 91

kūṭutal		108	91
		-------	-------
1. paṉṉīruyirum mutaṉmoḻiyāka varum eṉṟa niliyil innūlukkum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukkum māṟupāṭilli.
2. oḷaviyam pēcēl, kaṇṭoṉṟu collēl eṉṟa iruceyyuṭkum iṭiyil aḵkam curukkēl eṉṟa ceyyuḷ uḷḷatu. itaṉāl oḷaviyār uyirukkum meykkum naṭuvē āytattik koṇṭār eṉavum mutaleḻutti muppattoṉṟāka eṇṇiṉār eṉavum karutalām.
aṟinta veḻuttammuṉ paṉṉiraṇ ṭāvikaḷāṉa kammuṉ
piṟanta patiṉeṭṭu mey; naṭuvāytam 
eṉṟa vīracōḻiyam oḷaviyiṉ karuttukku ottiruttal kāṇka.
3. meyyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi paṟṟi ātticūṭiyil pala cikkalkaḷ uḷa. 14-31 variyuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭuc ceyyuṭkaḷ 18 meykaḷum moḻi mutalāka vantaṉa pōl eḻutappaṭṭavi. ivi nuṇukiya āyvukku uriya ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉi īṇṭu eṭutteḻutik koḷvatu nallatu.
1. 	kaṇṭoṉṟu collēl
2. 	ṅappōl vaḷi
3. 	caṉi nīrāṭu
4. 	ñayampaṭavuri
5. 	iṭampaṭa vīṭeṭēl
6. 	iṇakka maṟintiṇaṅku
7. 	tanti tāyppēṇ
8. 	naṉṟi maṟavēl
9. 	paruvattē payircey
10. 	maṉṟu paṟittuṇṇēl
11. 	iyalpalātaṉa ceyēl
12. 	aravamāṭṭēl
13. 	ilavam pañciṟṟuyil
14. 	vañcakam pēcēl
15. 	aḻakalātaṉa ceyēl
16. 	iḷamiyiṟkal
17. 	aṟaṉi maṟavēl
18. 	aṉantalāṭēl
ippatiṉeṭṭum kakaramutal ṉakara iṟutiyākavum, iṉaveḻuttu aṭuttaṭuttu varavum amintuḷa. neṭuṅkaṇakkuppaṭi ṟakaraṉakaram iṟutiyil uḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl patineṭṭu meyyiṉiyum mutaṉiliyāka oḷaviyār koṇṭār eṉṟu colluvativiṭa appatiṉeṭṭiṉiyum neṭuṅ kaṇakkuppaṭi niralpaṭa vittukkāṭṭa eṇṇiṉār eṉṟu koḷvatē poruttam. aṅṅaṉam kāṭṭiyavar moḻi mutalil vārā eṉṟu karutiya ṭa ṇa ya ra la ḻa ḷa ṟa ṉa eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyeḻuttukkaḷi oṟṟākavē vittuk kāṭṭiyirukkalām. iṭampaṭa, iṇakkam, aravam eṉa uyirmeyniliyil ēṉ kūṟiṉār eṉpatu teriyavilli. āṉāl patiṉeṭṭuc ceyyuṭkaḷilum uḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ ellām kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷām eṉpatum avaṟṟi uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttu, uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttu eṉa iruvakip paṭuttalām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttu oṉpatum (ka ṅa ca ña ta na pa ma va) uṇmiyāṉa moḻi mutaṉilikaḷām eṉpatum taṉi uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttuṭiya oṉpatum (iṭa, iṉa, iya, ara, ila, aḻa, iḷa, aṟa, aṉa) moḻi mutal vārātavaṟṟi neṭuṅ kaṇakku muṟippaṭi collutaṟkuk kiyāṇṭa neṟi eṉpatum teṟṟeṉappaṭum. in neṟikku akaram ikaram eṉṟa iru kuṟṟuyirit tavira vēṟu olikaḷik koḷḷavilli eṉpatum nōkkattakum.
ravviṟ kammuta lāmuk kuṟilum
valviṟ kimmutal iraṇṭum yavviṟku
iyyum moḻimuta lākimuṉ varumē
moḻi mutal vārāta meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkumuṉ mukkuṟil vantu moḻi mutalākum eṉṟa naṉṉūl vitiyiyum niṉika.
4. moḻi mutal varum ka ṅa ca ña ta na pa ma va eṉṟa oṉpatu meykaḷil ṅakaram taṉṉic collum vaḻi moḻi mutal vantatallatu oru collil vantataṉṟu. eṉavē ṅaṉam moḻi mutalām eṉṟa naṉṉūṟ koḷki oḷavikku illi. āṉāl ñakara mutaṉili paṟṟi ellā ilakkaṇa nūlukkum ātticūṭikkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
āe
o eṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya 	(tol. 31)
a ā e ovvo ṭāku ñammutal 	(naṉ. 105)
iṅṅaṉam tolkāppiyam ñakaram mūvuyirōṭum, naṉṉūl nāluyirōṭum moḻi mutalāki varum eṉṟu nūṟpittiruppavum, ñayampaṭavuri eṉṟu oru mutaṉ moḻiyē kūṟuvar oḷaviyār. ñakaramāvatu akarattōṭu kūṭi varum eṉa uṭaṉpaṭṭa ivar yakaram etaṟkum moḻi mutalākātu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟār. āvoṭu kūṭivarum eṉat tolkāppiyamum a ā u ū o oḷa eṉṟa āṟuyirōṭum varum eṉa vīracōḻiyamum naṉṉūlum kūṟiyiruppa, ātticūṭi yakarattukku oru mutaṉiliyum koṭātatu viyappāka uḷḷatu. ‘iyalpalātaṉa ceyēl’ eṉṟu moḻi mutalākā eḻuttukkaḷil oṉṟākak koṇṭu ikaravuyiri iṇikkiṉṟatu. eṉavē ka ca ta pa ña na ma va eṉṟa eṭṭu meykaḷē uṇmiyāka moḻi mutalāka varum eṉpatu oḷaviyiṉ eḻuttiyal.
5. muṉṉark kūṟiyapaṭi uyireḻuttu variciyum āytanaṭuvum vittapiṉ patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷukku oru varici vippatē oḷaviyiṉ nōkkam. itu vēṟoru vakiyālum teḷivupaṭum.
kaṇṭoṉṟu collēl 		... 	14
kaṭivatu maṟa 		... 	32
caṉi nīrāṭu 		... 	16
cakkara neṟinil 		... 	43
tanti tāyp pēṇ		... 	20
takkō ṉeṉattiri 		... 	54
naṉṟi maṟavēl 		... 	21
naṉmi kaṭippiṭi 		... 	65
paruvattē payircey 		... 	22
paḻippaṉa pakarēl 		... 	76
maṉṟu paṟittuṇṇēl 		... 	23
maṉantaṭumāṟēl 		... 	87
vañcakam pēcēl 		... 	27
vallami pēcēl 		... 	98
iṅṅaṉam kakaram mutal vakaram vari ovvoṉṟiṟkum iraṇṭu ātticūṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mutalāvatāka iruppavi taṉi meyvaricikkeṉa eḻutappaṭṭavi. iraṇṭāvatāka iruppavi ka kā ki kī eṉa varum uyir mey varicikku eḻutappaṭṭavi.
6. ātticūṭiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu amippu evvaḷavu cikkalāṉatu eṉṟu itukāṟum ceyta pākupāṭṭiliruntu ōraḷavu terintatu. itē oḷavi pāṭiya koṉṟi vēntaṉ neṭuṅkaṇakku variciyiṟ pāṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟālum taṉimeyvaricip patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷiyum mēṟkoḷḷa villi. uyirvarici āytanaṭu uyirmeyvarici eṉa mūṉṟē aṅku uḷḷaṉa. ātticūṭi nūṟṟeṭṭāka, koṉṟivēntaṉ 91 ākak kuṟintataṟkuk kāraṇamtaṉimey varici iṉmiyē. pāratiyār putiya ātticūṭiyilum pāratitācaṉ mutalātti cūṭiyilum āytattiyum taṉimey variciyiyum koḷḷa villi. iḷiyār ātticūṭiyil pāratitācaṉ āytattik koḷḷāviṭṭālum, 
kaṇakkil tērcci koḷ
cariyāy eḻutu
tamiḻ uṉ tāymoḻi
nallavaṉāy iru
palliṉit tūymi cey
maṟṟavarkku utavicey
vaṇṭi pārttu naṭa
eṉṟu eḻu taṉimey varici pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivarum oḷaviyārpōla uyirmeykkuṟilip piṉpaṟṟiyiruppatu niṉiyattakum.
7. uyir āytam mey eṉṟa variciyāyvukkuppiṉ, iṉi kakara uyirmey mutalāṉa iṉavaricikaḷi ārāyvōm. oḷaviyār karuttupapaṭi, moḻikku mutalil oṉpatu meykaḷ varum eṉiṉum ṅakaram taṉṉi eṭuttu moḻiyum aḷavil varuvatāl ēṉiyavi eṭṭē.. eṭṭiṉuḷḷum ñakaram ñayampaṭavuri eṉṟa aḷavil aṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. atu ña ñā ñi pōṉṟa iṉa varicikku uriyatilli eṉpatu oḷaviyār karuttup pōlum. ñaṇṭu-naṇṭu; ñāyiṟu-nāyiṟu; ñāti-nāti eṉṟu iṭikkālattup palkivanta vaḻakki nōkki ñakaraviṉatti matiyātu viṭṭārō oḷaviyār? yakaratti mutaṉ moḻiyākak koḷḷāmikkum ituvē kāraṇamāka irukkalām. yāṭu-āṭu, yāṉi-āṉi, yāṉi-āḷi, yuttam-uyuttam eṉa yakaram taṉnili iḻantu varutal kāṇka. tolkāppiyam yā oṉṟu moḻi mutal varum eṉakkūṟavum, vīracōḻiyam naṉṉūl ya yā yu yū yō yu eṉṟu āṟu moḻi mutalām eṉa virintiruppavum yakaram etuvum varātu eṉa avviṉattiyē oḷaviyār aṭiyōṭu oḻittirukkiṟār eṉpataṉi moḻivaralāṟṟāḷar uṇara vēṇṭum.
8. ṅakaram ñakaram yakaram immūṉṟiṉ tīyūḻ ātti cūṭiyil ivvāṟāka, ēṉi uyirmey varicikaḷum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukku ottaṉavāka illi. ka ta na pa ma va eṉṉum āṟu meyyum oḷakārattōṭu mutalākum eṉpar tolkāppiyar. ivaṟṟoṭu ca ya eṉṉum iraṇṭiyum kūṭṭi eṭṭumeyyoṭu oḷa mutalām eṉpar puttamittiraṉārum pavaṇantiyārum. oḷa eṉṟa uyirit taṉ peyarkku mutalākap peṟṟa nam oḷavi yārō eṉiṉ oḷaviyam pēcēl eṉṟu oḷakāram taṉiyuyirāka moḻi mutalāvataṉṟi enta meyyōṭum cērntu vārātu eṉṟu ilakkiyam ceytuviṭṭār. yakarattiṟkuc ceyta puraṭci pōṉṟatu itu. kavvi, tavvi, mavval, pavvam, navvi, cavukkiyam, vavvāl eṉṟu eḻutalām eṉpatu ivar karuttup pōlum. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkip piṉpaṟṟāmal makkaḷ vaḻakkiṟ kūṟum pēccoliyi muḻutum taḻuvik koṇṭārō oḷaviyār eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. mutaleḻuttil ivvaḷavu oḻivu ceytavar ātticūṭipōla īṟṟeḻuttukkum oru cūṭipāṭiyiruntāl, avar kālattiṉ oli vaḻakkiṉi evvaḷavō terintukoḷḷa muṭiyum. moḻi mutalāka uyirmey oḷakārattik koḷḷāmiyiṉāl kakaramutal makaram vari iṉavarici 11 āyiṉa. vakaramum muṉṉark kūṟiyapaṭi eṭṭu ākātu ēḻāyiṟṟu. koṉṟivēntaṉil orē oru vēṟupāṭu. ‘kuvi colliṉ evvarukkum paki’ eṉṟa ceyyuḷil kakaram oḷakārattōṭu mutalāki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
ātticūṭi 108
koṉṟivēntaṉ 108 - taṉimey 18=90 + ku = 91
9. moḻi mutal eḻuttukkaḷil palavaṟṟik kuṟitta oḷaviyār ātticūṭiyiṉ iṟutiyil vakaraviṉatti vikkumpōtu,
	vallami pēcēl
	vātumuṟ kūṟēl

	veṭṭeṉap pēcēl

eṉṟu 11 ceyyuṭkaḷ kūṟimuṭippar. ivaṟṟuḷ va vā vi vī ve vē vi eṉṟa ēḻumē vakaraviṉavarici mutalkaḷ. uttamaṉ, ūruṭaṉ, oṉṉār, ōram eṉṟa nāṉkum uyireḻuttukkaḷ. ivveḻuttukkaḷukku uṭiyatu viḷampēl, ūkkamatu kiviṭēl, oppuravoḻuku, ōtuvatoḻiyēl eṉṟu muṉpē cūṭikaḷ taṉiyuyir variciyil uḷa. mīṇṭum ivaṟṟukkuc cūṭipāṭuvatu evvakiyiṟ poruntum? kakaram mutal vakaram vari ellām 11 eṉṟa ōr oḻuṅku vēṇṭi ivvāṟu valintu ceytiruppārā? koṉṟivēntaṉilum ivvoḻuṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ vaḻakkil ukara ūkāra okara ōkāraṅkaṭku varakavoli iṇittup pēcuvatu uṇṭu. in nāṉkum itaḻ kuviyolikaḷ; vakaramō pallitaḻ iyiyap piṟappatu. itaḻiyipukkum itaḻ kuvivukkum nerukkam uṇṭu. ātaliṉ makkaḷ vāyolikku matippuk koṭutta oḷavip perumāṭṭi ātticūṭiyi ēṭṭil eḻutiya kāli innāṉku cūṭikaḷiyum,
	vuttamaṉāyiru
	vūruṭaṉ kūṭivāḻ
	voṉṉārit tēṟēl
	vōrañ collēl
eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppārō? piṉṉar itu iḻivaḻakku, moḻikku aḻivaḻakku eṉṟu eṇṇi eḻutuvōrāl viṭappaṭṭatu pōlum. 108 eṉṟa oru meypporuḷ eṇṇikkikkāka ivvāṟu cērttiruppār eṉṟu karuta iṭamilli. avvāṟu eṇṇiyirupparēl ñakaraviṉattiyō yakaraviṉattiyō taḻuviyiruttal eḷitākavum muṟiyākavum irukkum. koṉṟivēntaṉ 91 āka iruttaliṉ oḷavi 108 nampikkiyuṭiyavar illi eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.


13. moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varampu
tamiḻmoḻikku uriya eḻuttukkaḷ ittuṇiya eṉavum avi uyirmey, cārpu eṉṟa perumpirivum, kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭiyiṉam eṉṟa uṭpirivum paṭum eṉavum ellā ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum toṭakkattē kaṟpippatik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ataṉoṭu amiyātu coṟku mutalāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṟutiyāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṭinili mayakkāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉavāṅku mutalīṟiṭinilikaḷ paravalākac cuṭṭappaṭuvatiyum kāṇkiṟōm. ciṟu vakuppukkut tamiḻ payiṟṟum ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilum mutal īṟu iṭinili paṟṟiya varampukaḷ iṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. ayalnāṭṭavarkkuk kaṟpikkum payiṟci pōṭṭikaḷilum im muttaṉmikaḷ olikaḷiṉ āyvoṭu viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tolkāppiyam mutalāvatu nūṉmarapil iṭinili mayakkattik kūṟi iraṇṭāvatu moḻi marapil mutaleḻuttu īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷi virivāka iyampum.  naṉṉūl eḻuttiyalil mutalnili iṭinili iṟuti nili mūṉṟiyum ivvariciyiṟ kūṟum vīracōḻiya mutalāṉa nūlkaḷum im marapip pōṟṟi moḻikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mutalīṟu iṭi eṉṟa  mukkūṟu eḻuttilakkaṇa vitikaṭkellām aṭimayam eṉpatu oruvāṟu uṇarappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ moḻi mutaleḻuttiṉ varalāṟum vaḷarcciyum varampum etir nōkkum paṟṟi ik kaṭṭuriyil cila neṟikaḷi ārāyvōm.

	ippaṭṭiyaṟ kāṭciyiliruntu aṟiyat takuvaṉa :
a. vēṟupāṭiṉmi : (80 eḻuttukkaḷ)
1.  paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum mutaṉ moḻiyātal
2. katanapama ellāvuyiroṭum varutal
3. vakaram (u ū o ō nīṅkalāka) eṭṭuyiroṭum varutal
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻp pērakarātiyilum māṟā vittaṉmiyik kāṇalām. ay, av eṉṟa pōlikku mutaṉmi koṭuttu, ai, oḷa eṉṉum neṭilkaḷi vilakka villi eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakum. ivvāṟu mūvāyiram āṇṭukku mēlākat tolkāppiyar kāla mutal oru peṟṟit tāka innalvaravi aṟikiṟōm.
ā. vēṟupāṭu
cakarameymmutal 	tolkāppiyam 9 (a, ai, oḷa 									nīṅkalāka)
					ilakkaṇa viḷakkam 10 (ai, oḷa nīṅkalāka)
					tamiḻnūl 10 			(mēṟpaṭi)
					piṟa ellā nūlkaḷilum 12 uyiroṭum 						moḻi mutalākum.
ceṉṉit tamiḻp pērakarātiyil ca mutal cu vari uṇṭu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkamum tamiḻnūlum tamakku muṟpaṭṭa vīracōḻiyam nēminātam naṉṉūl mutaliyavaṟṟip piṉpaṟṟavilli. aḵtāvatu ciyum cuvum mutalāvati uṭaṉpaṭṭila.

cāminātamum tamiḻnūlum mēl kīḻ muraṇākavuḷa. tamiḻp pērakarātiyil cāminātam pōl ñakaram āṟuyirōṭum mutalākum.
yakara meymmutal
tolkāppiyam 	(1) 	ā
vīracōḻiyam 	(6) 	a, ā, u, ū, o, oḷa
nēminātam 		(3) 	ā, u, ō
naṉṉūl 		(6) 	a, ā, u,ū, ō, oḷa
i. viḷakkam 	(1) 	ā
cāminātam 		(6) 	a, ā, u, ū, ō, oḷa
muttuvīriyam 	(6) 	a, ā, u, ū, ō, oḷa
tamiḻnūl 		(1) 	ā
ilakkaṇaviḷakkamum tamiḻnūlum iṭippaṭṭa nūl kaḷic cārātu tolkāppayivaḻic celkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻp pērakarāti vīracōḻiya naṉṉūl pōla yakarattukku āṟuyir koḷḷum. yippōtu, yeppōtu eṉac cila eḻuttuvaḻakku iruppiṉum avi ēṟkappaṭa villi.
tolkāppiyam kūṟiya nunti eṉṉum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram piṉṉar vīḻcciyāyiṟṟu. ivvilakkaṇatti uṭaṉpaṭṭāl meymmutaṟ puṇarcci eṉṟa nili illātu pōkum eṉṟu mayilinātar oru pāṅku maṟutturippar. naṉṉūlār koḻukompupaṭa moḻinta ṅakara mutaṉmoḻiyum piṉṉar kaṭipōka villi. ‘avvi oṭṭi ṅavvum mutalākum’ eṉṟa tiṟaṉil naṭiyē ṅakaram ēṉi meykaḷukku nikarāṉataṉṟu eṉpatikkāṭṭum eṉac caṅkaranamaccivāyar teḷivu paṭuttiyuḷḷār.
uriyāciriyarkaḷ karuttu
mēṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuḷ cakara ñakara yakara mutaṉmoḻi kaḷiṭi ilakkaṇikaḷ vēṟupaṭak kāraṇam yātu? ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal vitiyākaliṉ, ilakkiyam iruppavum vēṟupaṭal takumā? uḷḷatip paṭṭāṅku uṭaṉpaṭuvatu ilakkaṇamā? uḷḷatit tiṟaṉāyntu uṟuvatu cīrtūkkuvatu ilakkaṇamā? ivi vampāṉa viṉākkaḷ alla. karuttu vēṟṟumi matikkum viṉākkaḷ.
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum caṅkap paṉuvalkaḷ, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali, cintāmaṇi, peruṅkati, teyvappācuraṅkaḷ mutalāṉavaṟṟil putiya moḻi mutalkaḷ palavāṟu pukuntu kiṭappati aṟiyātārallar.
yūkamoṭu māmuka mucukkali 	(muruku. 300)
yūpam naṭṭa viyaṉkaḷam palakol 	(puṟam. 15)
yūpa neṭuntūṇ vēta vēḷvi 	(puṟam. 224)

talitumin teñciya poṉmaliyūpamoṭu 	(patiṟ. 67)
uñciyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya yūkiyantaṇaṉ 	(maṇi. 15)
